All revealed development/heldout seeds now exposed; none count as fresh future holdout. A different research hypothesis (e.g. recovery-aware progress or explicit mode transition/fall/get-up/resume) requires separate owner-approved roadmap and frozen protocol, not retroactive changes to H1; Branch B stays HOLD. Owner-only executable modifications remain in force. This SA commit updates Rules only.

### M4.70-H2-RAHC — RECOVERY-AWARE HYBRID CONTROL: OWNER-APPROVED ROADMAP, DESIGN ONLY (v1.2.39; 2026-11-24; A ONLY)

**Scope and authority:** Owner requested to proceed with redesigning the Recovery-Aware Control roadmap after H1 closure. This opens DESIGN of the distinct H2-RAHC research hypothesis within Branch A (not automatic activation of any earlier provisional H2 curriculum, and not reopening H1). H1 M1/M2 NO-PROMOTE, +100M NO-PROMOTE, +400M HOLD and Branch B HOLD remain unchanged. No source edits, simulator runs, new PPO, training budget, heldout evaluation or B implementation approved by this design step. Owner manually edits/pushes any eventual source; SA reviews exact SHA first. All numerical H2A design constants below are provisional engineering starting values, to be frozen in a reviewed owner source/protocol before any outcome is observed; changes after first outcome count as a new separately authorized hypothesis, not tuning until PASS.

**Evidence and hypothesis:** H1 final 180-rollout audit (v1.2.38): 61/61 source ON terminations foot-foot; M2 raw anticipation before selected collisions and actual PPO activation, but no net development survival gain against OLD10M, heldout0/12 and nominal tracking failures. 26/90 prepush observable mismatches and unknown sampled source push magnitude limit causal claims. These do not prove reward shaping cannot work, nor that foot contact is always the initiating cause. Hypothesis H2A: a bounded, state-dependent command supervisor applied to unchanged C401M can improve upright recovery and return to the original command by allowing temporary compensatory movement; this isolates an inference-time control strategy before any new learning. It is a command governor, NOT a true capture-point stepping controller, not proof of stability or formal novelty. If it fails, stop the bounded test and reconsider control authority or a separately approved recovery-policy design instead of adding more H1 penalties. Baseline C401M frozen SHA `1ccf579ea35d4518713b00ea20d77db8463bc469b7de903b8f10f457b3dc90b8`; OLD10M/M1/M2 archival only for context, no new baseline promotion.

**Literature anchors (external context, not reproduction or results for this repo):** Pratt et al., Capture Point: A Step toward Humanoid Push Recovery, Humanoids 2006, DOI 10.1109/ICHR.2006.321385; https://xplorestaging.ieee.org/document/4115602 ; Poddar et al., Embedding Classical Balance Control Principles in Reinforcement Learning for Humanoid Recovery, arXiv:2603.08619 (2026 preprint, https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08619); HumanUP and HoST RSS2025 https://www.roboticsproceedings.org/rss21/p063.html and https://www.roboticsproceedings.org/rss21/p064.html for long-term context only, no Branch B task opened. LIP capture point xi=p_COM+v_COM/sqrt(g/h) is a simplified constant-height/limited-angular-momentum model; do not call pelvis location true COM, static support polygon a complete dynamic viability test, or command bias a guaranteed foot placement. Optional COM/foot contact diagnostic in future only if current sensor data and action relevance justify it, not prerequisite microprobe.

**H2A architecture and first single independent factor:** External supervisor OFF vs ON, both exact same frozen C401M actor, normalizer, pinned MJX/PPO/source observation/action, source PD/reward/termination and deterministic policy key semantics. Maintain immutable goal command c_goal and independent effective c_eff; patch command into BOTH actor/critic obs[9:12] and info.command consistently using existing verified helper. Evaluate tracking ONLY against c_goal; never count reducing the command as improving tracking. Use causal current observation velocity and projected gravity (validated layout, units, normalized upright sign and timing), no push event flag, future states, OFF trace, terminal label or oracle simulator impulse as controller inputs. Simulator-only contact/height/CoM may be logged for analysis, not fed into this first controller. Trigger based on e=norm(v_xy_obs-c_goal_xy) and theta=angle of normalized projected gravity to verified upright reference. Proposed enter if e>=0.45 m/s OR theta>=20 deg for 2 consecutive observed control ticks; recovery exit if e<=0.20 m/s AND theta<=10 deg for 10 ticks. With 50Hz control: 2 ticks=.04s, 10=.20s. Hysteresis and timeout needed; RECOVERY active at most 100 ticks (2s), then bounded RETURN, timeout recorded as failed intervention, never artificial episode reset/termination. During recovery c_eff_xy = clip_source(c_goal_xy + clip_norm(0.5*(v_xy_obs-c_goal_xy),0.25 m/s)); c_eff_yaw target=0.5*c_goal_yaw. Source command ranges confirmed in pinned joystick x[-1,1],y[-.5,.5],yaw[-1,1]. Limit command change per control tick to 0.05 m/s vector norm and 0.10 rad/s yaw; interpolate back toward original command using same limits, log return, no teleport or action discontinuity introduced deliberately. NORMAL exact c_eff=c_goal; initial mode NORMAL and counters reset; RETURN can reenter on risk, with total intervention window bounded at 100 ticks plus <=10 ticks return, timeout lockout until risk-clear dwell (prevents unbounded recovery reentry). State must be included in forked snapshot; no changes to network input dimensions or PPO. Positive velocity bias is a hypothesis to encourage a step in direction of drift, not damping or guaranteed balancing; may worsen drift/turning. Reject if no effective control or nominal false activation. H2A source review must settle exact filter/timing/return bound and ensure qvel kick does not grant supervisor earlier refreshed sensor information than baseline.

**Reuse, not duplicate evaluator:** Read `m463_gate3_cp_eval.py` blob `157962b1fffd599b45c0c5619fe641f86db2e94c` at v1.2.38, existing full state fork at push_step-1, one_step patches command/obs, deterministic actor, matched_off_recovery (vx/vy/yaw/height/tilt bands 0.1m/s/0.1m/s/0.1rad/s/0.05m/5deg for 10 consecutive steps), fixed_window_tracking requiring both traces reach horizon. Existing source is a base, not yet supports H2: future owner minimal opt-in supervisor ON/OFF in existing evaluator (rather than fake candidate training report or new runner), use exact same policy instance/checkpoint for both, add supervisor state to snapshot, log original/effective command, mode/risk/action, support contacts/height/tilt, event step/exact applied world delta, terminal cause, return timing, actual corrective foot contact timing when measurable (not every contact = corrective), keep defaults unchanged. Freeze identical pre-first-push state, RNG and control prefix for both arms and matched OFF; no intervention before push in nominal prefix. Second push scheduled step335, compute fixed world direction from shared pre-first-push yaw and apply EXACT same world delta across arms; within each arm fork its own single-push state at334 into double; cannot claim identical second state across controllers. Verify sensor latency and same physical injection/observation timing in both arms, no future information. This design explicitly differs from prior body-relative second-kick/source-randomized tests; preserve those historical metrics. Source push disabled only in fixed-kick eval to avoid double stacking; perturbation = world XY root delta-v in m/s, NOT force in N. No modifications to existing trainer/environment in H2A.

**Bounded roadmap and stop gates (future execution requires reviewed source/protocol and explicit owner GO):**
- H2A0 — one consolidated owner source/technical gate: manual edit of existing evaluator only after SA supplies precise reviewed diff; static/source pin, supervisor OFF compatibility, same checkpoint and prefix, deterministic fixed delta-v injection, bounded command/state/finite telemetry, original goal metrics, validated observation sign/units and timeout/return semantics; include this in one integrated CUDA/MJX job with H2A1 when approved, not repetitive microprobes. No PPO. Current task ends at roadmap design, no executable changes yet.
- H2A1 — one fixed development comparison: 2 previously exposed dev seeds [7331,7357] x 3 existing profiles [stand,forward_fast,turn_left] x directions [left,right] =12 paired perturbation cases; OFF 6 unique per arm, SINGLE push step300 delta-v0.50m/s, DOUBLE same first push plus step335 delta-v0.35m/s; max1000 steps, exact same C401M both arms, 2 x (6 nominal +12 single +12 double)=60 logical trajectories (shared prefixes may be reused, not extra duplicate rollouts). If terminated before second, count double case as failure/not exposed and report; no conditioning primary denominator on survival to second push. No need to rerun old M435 H1. Use existing development scenarios only; no claim statistically independent. Recovery success by2s/5s after last scheduled push requires survived corresponding horizon AND ten consecutive ticks within existing matched-OFF five-variable bands AND actual tracking of original goal (proposed XY command error norm <=.20m/s and yaw error <=.30rad/s) AND c_eff returned to c_goal for those ticks. Record recovery time and later termination separately; no survivor-only RMSE or conflating horizon censoring with recovery. Require all nominal6/6 each, candidate supervisor no nominal intervention (exact command equality), per-case nominal XY/yaw nonregression <=5% relative when baseline >=.01 in corresponding units, otherwise absolute increase <=.0005; no new critical terminal type/nonfinite or joint limit/actuator violation relative to source. H2A1 directional screen: candidate DOUBLE survival gain >=2/12 AND double recovery-by5s count gain >=2/12 vs supervisor OFF; no lost SINGLE survival case; no new nominal regression. Report gains/losses, all 2s/5s counts, comparable windows, initial exposure, command deviations and exact injected deltas. If screen FAIL/INCONCLUSIVE close H2A1, no automatic threshold tuning or PPO; return design decision to owner.
- H2A2 — only if H2A1 passes and separately approved, freeze controller unchanged; before any heldout evaluation choose and audit 4 truly unused reset seeds (all previously exposed H1/H2A development seeds excluded) and freeze expanded profile/direction/phase/dose schedule before results. Compare OFF/ON same C401M with equal fixed kick magnitude/timing; nominal, single/repeated recovery, recovery return and tracking, then one bounded randomized/source-push robustness validation if owner approves protocol. Do not use heldout to tune or call independent without audit. Freeze sample counts, numeric gates and total evaluation budget in subsequent design approval; no GPU/learning authorization now. A successful no-training supervisor is a candidate only after heldout/robustness pass; no need to add RL just for novelty.
- H2B — separate future design only if evidence shows command interface inadequate or learning is necessary: evaluate structured corrective-step target / physics-guided residual recovery controller with isolated supervision and frozen locomotion actor; potentially learn a dedicated recovery policy with mode-conditioned objective and return-to-goal outcome, compare against H2A and equal-budget no-recovery control. Choose ONE mechanism, explicit design and finite budget before any owner-authorized smoke or independent pilot; no blanket gating, no automatic +10M or continuation from M1/M2. No implementation or budget approved here.
- Long-term North Star unchanged: get-up and integrated resume in Branch B ONLY after explicit owner request; current roadmap does not start F0/F1/F2/F3/F4, change termination or authorize fallen-state training. Upright recovery return to goal is distinct from post-fall resume.

**Current decision:** `H2_RAHC_ROADMAP_DESIGN_COMPLETE / H2A0_OWNER_SOURCE_DESIGN_PENDING`. Next deliver precise existing-evaluator source design/owner diff and protocol review, then ONE consolidated read-only-in-weights fixed-kick evaluation if explicitly authorized. No GPU or PPO executed, no executable repository changes by SA. Design is not performance evidence.

### M4.70-H2A0 — OWNER SOURCE DESIGN HANDOFF, NO EXECUTION GO (v1.2.40; 2026-11-24)

Task H2A0 / PUSH and upright RESUME only. Reviewed exact evaluator `m463_gate3_cp_eval.py` blob `157962b1fffd599b45c0c5619fe641f86db2e94c` at HEAD `7a0ab79987f100b3b9858a51b14f1fb95434d775`, and exact upstream `google-deepmind/mujoco_playground` joystick.py at PIN `9bcbe736177472ba35d60773e3030a36e37962b7`. Actor observation state indices: noisy local pelvis linear velocity [0:3], gyro [3:6], projected gravity [6:9] computed with world [0,0,-1] (upright reference NEGATIVE z; distinguish from torso termination gravity sensor), command [9:12], joint pos [12:41], joint vel [41:70], last action [70:99], phase [99:103]. Privileged begins with same actor state. Use only current actor observation for risk/control, same noise and latency as C401M. Do not feed simulator contact, force, truth velocity or kick schedule to governor. Source eval diagnostics use post-step true pelvis velocity, torso tilt, fixed original goal; report sensor vs truth distinction. No model/PD/reward/termination/observation shape change; only existing command override value changes.

Owner manual edit ONLY existing evaluator: opt-in `--h2a` branch, require checkpoint C only and exact dev protocol (seeds7331,7357; profiles stand,forward_fast,turn_left; directions left,right; first300/.50,second335/.35,max1000); legacy C/P CLI and output untouched unless flag. Load C once, use existing one_step diagnostics with optional effective command parameter, set both obs arrays [9:12] and info.command; compute goal errors against fixed original command. H2A-only post-step action and source model limit telemetry; check limited joints/actuator ctrl/force according to actual model masks/ranges; report excess and compare to baseline with explicit tolerances (joint1e-3 rad, ctrl1e-3 in model units, force0.1 in model units), not proof of hardware safety. Existing source terminal categories unchanged. No extra source file or automatic code rewriting. Static syntax/plan only after manual edit, owner push exact HEAD, SA review before GPU.

Governor pure host scalar logic with immutable-valued per-branch state: NORMAL, RECOVERY, RETURN, LOCKOUT. e=norm(actor velocity_xy-goal_xy); normalized projected gravity angle to negative z; reject nonfinite or degenerate gravity. Risk e>=.45 OR tilt>=20, clear e<=.20 AND tilt<=10; enter after2, return after10 clear; gain.5, norm bias<=.25, yaw target=.5*goal, source limits x[-1,1],y[-.5,.5],yaw[-1,1], slew XY norm<=.05/tick, yaw<=.10/tick. Intervention age persists through RECOVERY/RETURN reentries, forced return at budget100 ticks, bound extra return<=10 ticks, timeout logged once, LOCKOUT at original goal until 10 fresh clear ticks; no automatic reset/termination. OFF exactly goal. Record risk, state, age, timeout, effective/goal command, action, contacts, source safety observations each tick. Recovery success requires full 2s/5s window alive, ten consecutive post-event ticks within five matched nominal bands plus original-goal XY<=.20, yaw<=.30, governor NORMAL and effective=goal; timeout separately reported. No claim that all new foot contacts are corrective.

Pairing: run one truly shared prefix per seed/profile through step299 with real ON governor shadow evaluation on current observations, require zero activation and exact effective goal before each common transition; if prepush activation/termination, retain partial evidence and stop as nominal/pairing NO-GO, never mask risk using push flag. Branch OFF/ON nominal and perturbed runs from same full MJX snapshot and copied supervisor state (OFF governor disabled); preserve RNG and exact first world delta from shared yaw. At step334 snapshot each arm's single trajectory and supervisor state for its double branch; second world direction from shared FIRST yaw and exact delta same across both arms. Kick modifies root qvel only, no refresh of actor obs for either arm; both first act on previous observation, record timing. No-exposure double due to early single failure counted as failure with observed prefix, not excluded. Raw records allow reconstruction of nominal/single/double full paths and all source causes; retain first/second deltas, frame global step/done and original/effective commands. Plan 6 groups x2 arms x(1 nominal+2 single+2 double)=60 logical trajectories, with shared prefixes counted once physically. No H1 replay or PPO. One consolidated runtime gate + bounded evaluation only after source review and separate explicit GO. H2A1 previously locked directional performance gates and H2A2/Branch B hold unchanged. Design source handoff is not source review PASS, GPU PASS, or efficacy.

Current `H2A0_OWNER_EDIT_PENDING`. No executable repo modifications, GPU or training by SA. Rollback only owner manual revert of own evaluator diff; never alter pinned source/checkpoints.

### M4.70-H2B-R0 — SUPPORT-AWARE CAPTURE POINT COMMAND RESIDUAL, OWNER-APPROVED DESIGN ROADMAP (v1.2.45; 2026-11-24)

Owner requested continuation after H2A1 NO-GO, explicitly per Rules. This authorizes H2B research design, not executable source writes by SA, GPU execution, PPO, or retroactive H2A alteration. H1 CLOSED, H2A1 CLOSED, H2A2 not eligible. Re-read v1.2.44 evidence and exact evaluator blob e26043088bee558015b8592b6ef86dd85bf546b1 at HEAD 8cf1a6218551c07f46f9bf6500ec3a93fdf718ad. H2A1 failed nominal startup before push, partial stand results show zero survival/recovery gain and joint-limit regression; neither proves all command interfaces incapable. H2B hypothesis: state/contact/phase-conditioned departure from nominal capture-point trajectory is more relevant than absolute velocity tracking error, and a bounded command residual in that direction MAY improve recovery using frozen C401M. Treat detector+mapping as one candidate mechanism, no component causal claims. No joint action residual, new policy, reward gating, or IK at this stage.

**Technical basis and limits:** Pratt et al., Capture Point: A Step toward Humanoid Push Recovery (2006), https://xplorestaging.ieee.org/document/4115602 ; Englsberger/Ott/Albu-Schäffer, Three-Dimensional Bipedal Walking Control Based on Divergent Component of Motion (2015), https://elib.dlr.de/96741/ . LIPM xi_xy = c_xy + v_com_xy/sqrt(g/h), h positive CoM height above floor, g=9.81. Raw capture point outside instantaneous support polygon is NOT a fall classifier for walking; commanded walking deliberately evolves support. Use support-center-relative DCM proxy d=R_yaw^T[(c_xy-s_xy)+v_com_xy/sqrt(g/h)] (metres) in heading frame. Center s of actually contacting foot geom center(s); both feet midpoint, single stance that foot, neither foot midpoint explicitly labelled NO_SUPPORT_PROXY, not a support region or CoP. No claim of exact capture region, dynamic feasibility or hardware estimator validation.

**Estimator design constraints:** exact pinned joystick.py/base.py reviewed: 103 actor obs, 29 action, source noisy local pelvis linvel (noise scale .1), gyro, gravity, joint pos/vel, phase; source contacts via feet geoms and floor, source foot force sensors and pelvis IMU site exist. Existing evaluator has foot geometry, contact, phase, joint/ctrl/force limit and original-goal diagnostics, no verified full CoM estimator yet. Implement only after owner handoff; use model body masses and kinematically available inertial-center positions (MJX xipos candidate, verify shape/availability), relative pelvis IMU site origin and orientation, feet geometry and contact proxies, not force schedule, future state, matched counterfactual, or exact injected delta as control input. Full-body CoM relative to pelvis site is mass-weighted sum over bodies with positive mass (world body excluded); model/FK plus idealized encoder/attitude/contact proxies in sim. v_com estimate = actor noisy local pelvis velocity rotated to world using same sampled site attitude plus backward finite difference of world-oriented pelvis-relative CoM across consecutive samples / .02; reset derivative zero explicitly logged and included in calibration, no startup suppression. Ground-relative height from idealized model/flat-floor geometry, effective h clipped [.35,1.0]m for numerical LIPM proxy, log raw/clipped and reject nonfinite/nonpositive h. Foot centers not sole polygon; no-contact reference category separately logged. Snapshot/cache sensor sample AND prior estimator/supervisor state from actual reset or previous completed physics step, before any kick; both arms act first on same cached pre-kick sample, do not read altered qvel on injection step, no new observation/RNG sampling. All current data fields verified in later source/runtime review, no claim implementation tested. Ideal simulator kinematics/contact/attitude are sensor-feasible PROXIES, not physical measured sensor estimate, not actor-only; deployability requires separate noisy encoder/IMU/contact estimation validation. Keep original actor noise, dimensions, normalizer, PD, physics/termination unchanged; no modification upstream.

**Proposed fixed nominal reference (freeze before any data):** one calibration set of six no-push, supervisor-disabled C401M trajectories: seeds [7301,7303] (development/calibration ONLY, not heldout; no freshness claim), profiles stand/forward_fast/turn_left, max1000, same pinned MJX runtime. For each profile and contact mode (left/right/both/neither proxy), bucket by command age ticks [0,10),[10,30),[30,75),[75,infinity) and left gait phase quadrants over [-pi,pi); use current causal age/phase, never kick time or arbitrary warm-up disable. Store componentwise min/max of d within bucket, deterministic minimum8 finite samples. If insufficient, back off to same profile/contact/age pooled phase; then same profile/contact pooled age+phase. No cross-profile/contact fallback. If no supported bucket, log unavailable and close as estimator/reference INCONCLUSIVE (do not silently label safe or tune from evaluation). Calibration must survive all six, finite. Freeze bucket counts/bounds and reference SHA before candidate evaluation, no fitting from evaluation data, no additional calibration runs. These are design choices, not proven thresholds. Reference is deliberately descriptive, not stability proof; broad envelope can miss disturbances. Apply to constant-command cases only; command-age feature is not an enable gate. Include calibration raw traces, provenance and exact fit artifact, no policy weights updated.

**Candidate H2B-P1 control:** for d and frozen rectangle B=[lower,upper], residual r = d - componentwise_clip(d,B), score rho=norm(r) in metres. Risk rho>=.04m, clear rho<=.015m, consecutive entry2/exit10 ticks. Use existing bounded NORMAL/RECOVERY/RETURN/LOCKOUT concept (new opt-in H2B, legacy H2A immutable), max100 active ticks, forced return<=10 ticks, no hidden push flag; no-contact category is a proxy not a proof of balance. Recovery target c_eff_xy=c_goal_xy+clip_norm(2.0 s^-1 * r,.25 m/s); source component bounds x[-1,1],y[-.5,.5], yaw unchanged at original goal (no separate yaw gain). XY slew <=.05m/s/tick; original yaw goal constant; exact goal NORMAL/LOCKOUT. Original goal used for all efficacy metrics. Sign is exploratory: shifting locomotion command toward excess DCM may elicit a step toward disturbance, may also amplify drift. Not direct foot-placement or certified stabilizing feedback. Record d, reference/bucket/backoff, rho, r, risk/state, estimator inputs/height, contacts, phase, actual foot placement/contact, effective/goal commands, all source safety metrics and timeouts. No claim every contact corrective. No post-result thresholds/coefficients/reference changes within this bounded experiment.

**Roadmap and finite experiment:** H2B0 owner source design handoff/review first: exact estimator and history cache/snapshot, reference serialization, deterministic pure logic test cases including startup, coordinate frames, contact switches, stale pre-kick measurement; reuse m463_gate3_cp_eval.py opt-in branch, preserve H2A source, no new executable file unless justified/approved. Owner manually edits/pushes, SA reviews SHA. H2B1 only separate explicit owner GO after source review: one integrated calibration+controlled evaluation, max6 calibration+60 paired logical trajectories (max49014 actual transitions with shared prefixes), no PPO. Calibration fit/hash before evaluation. Evaluation seeds7331,7357 (exposed dev), same 3 profiles/directions left/right, first300/.50, second335/.35, max1000, fixed world deltas, full shared prefix and per-arm single/double snapshot with estimator/controller history, OFF frozen C401M, ON frozen C401M+H2B-P1. Preserve true causal shadow governor in shared prefix; prepush intervention/termination NO-GO, unsupported reference technical/design INCONCLUSIVE, preserve evidence and STOP. No masking startup, no retry/recalibration/tuning. Require nominal6/6 each, zero nominal intervention (including startup) and per-case XY/yaw RMSE <=5% relative or <=.0005 absolute increase for baseline <.01; no new critical source terminal/nonfinite or poststep joint/ctrl/force limit regression using existing .001/.001/.1 tolerances. Double survival AND recovery-by5s gain each >=2/12, no lost single survival case, same matched original-goal recovery 2s/5s definition and bands, second push nonexposure counted failure. Report all paired cases, timeouts, tracking, contacts/foot placement, known limits; compare H2A stand partial descriptively only, no H2A replay. H2B2 only on full H2B1 PASS and separate owner-approved fresh holdout audit/protocol; otherwise close H2B-P1, no automatic parameter tuning or new budget. Future direct foot-placement/IK or physics-guided action residual/learning requires new explicit hypothesis and finite protocol; no implied PPO. Branch B remains HOLD; M1/M2/+100M NO-PROMOTE,+400M HOLD.

Decision H2B_R0_ROADMAP_DESIGN_COMPLETE / H2B0_SOURCE_DESIGN_PENDING. All parameters are proposed pre-data, exact implementation contract subject to pre-execution source design review (no post-result changes). Zero GPU/zero PPO/source edits by SA. Rules-only.

### M4.70-H2C-R0 — PREDICTIVE MOMENTUM INNOVATION, OWNER-APPROVED RESEARCH DESIGN ONLY (v1.2.52; 2026-11-24)

Read AGENTS, v1.2.51 including latest state/ledger/changelog and exact M463 evaluator blob65f0b46cc0b61561e8f75864ed9348be60a7919c at 7c5c5fe645ed67125c65d35d9fa3928eba5bd71d. No source edits, GPU, PPO, H2B retry or controller gain/threshold tuning. C401M and all baseline pins unchanged. Separate disturbance detection from recovery control; no inference of C401M recovery command authority or new network need from H2A/B failures.

**OBSERVED offline diagnostic, exploratory only:** Verified H2B raw JSON SHA256 7f6df94799aeb624c6b07f6f4b7b051d615a5cab27d5fc6a077ea07e16730337. 5994 within-trajectory consecutive preaction cached transitions (six x 999, never cross reset), no push labels. Predict delta estimated CoM XY velocity in previous heading frame R_k^T(v_{k+1}-v_k), persistence delta=0, LIPM prior b=.02 omega_k^2 R_k^T(c_k-support_k) if any contact, else zero. One exploratory 46-feature affine ridge correction to b: concatenate support-relative XY, XY estimated velocity, height_used, row3 of IMU rotation, relative_com_rate_world z, L/R contacts, phase sin/cos, log1p(age), original goal3, actual action29 of same transition (no poststep features); fit standardized training features, zero-variance sd->1 for sd<1e-9; intercept unpenalized, penalty .01*N for standardized coefficients. Leave entire seed out (train7301/test7303 then reverse); per profile vector RMSE persistence/LIPM/ridge (m/s): test7301 stand .15034/.15080/.10896, fast .14132/.14102/.10267, turn .14028/.14013/.10638; test7303 stand .14893/.14955/.10538, fast .14366/.14376/.11007, turn .14031/.14066/.10890. Ridge max errors .32865,.22723,.25182,.41487,.40968,.40755 respectively; startup75 stand7301 ridge .17421 vs persistence .15713. Fit both old seeds once for known failed7331 stand age0->1 diagnostic ONLY: error norms .10753/.11070/.23762 respectively. No fitting to seed7331. No new holdout or proof of detection; regression is offline identification, not PPO. NO-GO for promoting these predictors into supervisor: average improvement fails to resolve startup uncertainty, no real pushed CoM trace; H2B calibration lacks measured external contact impulses, true substep timing and full kinematics for force-balance identification. No threshold sweep or additional fit.

**Source and research basis:** Rotella et al. Humanoid Momentum Estimation Using Sensed Contact Wrenches (2015), https://arxiv.org/abs/1507.04401 ; MuJoCo 3.2.x XML force sensor semantics https://mujoco.readthedocs.io/en/3.2.3/XMLreference.html and 3.2.7 contact frame https://mujoco.readthedocs.io/en/3.2.7/computation/index.html . Exact pinned Playground base.py fd59950822eb11c4d17d970cbca8fda9de7d2a6d, joystick.py 561a18948e3467ab924468eaf362150dadb9d4a6 lines312–337,420+,575–583, g1_mjx_feetonly.xml 4a7c00cdc92e245914d39b86b69b9329a26c35f5 lines4,147,457–479, mjx_env.py 916c2f412a7a12568283f68eeb755e5d94ad9c83 lines158–169,267–269: Euler .002s, control .02s, 10 substeps, floating base, actual actor target default_pose+action*action_scale; source has pelvis accelerometer/velocimeter/gyro and two foot force sensors. XML force sensors measure child-parent interaction in local site frame, not automatically GRF; do NOT sum as GRF without validated dynamics conversion. MuJoCo tag3.2.7 MJX support.py 57c9475bca1a8641f05525da15190c93102a085c contact_force(m,d,contact_id,to_world_frame), contact_force_dim; forward.py 07f33a90c8ed68bc545ba47bfb78d4bfb8db1f52 forward() then euler(), contact/kinematic/sensor fields may correspond to preintegration state. Exact installed API/time/sign and integrator numerical error still unverified. Source links https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco_playground/tree/9bcbe736177472ba35d60773e3030a36e37962b7/mujoco_playground/_src/locomotion/g1 and https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco/blob/3.2.7/mjx/mujoco/mjx/_src/support.py .

**Selected H2C-P1 new hypothesis (simulation-only diagnostic observer, no action modification):** input-driven centroidal linear momentum balance. For robot mass M, p=M*v_com, p_hat[k+1]=p_hat_measured[k]+M*g*dt+J_ground[k], J_ground=sum_{j=0..9} dt_sim*sum F_ground_world[j], horizontal innovation e=R_yaw[k]^T(v_measured[k+1]-v_measured[k]-J_ground_xy[k]/M) in m/s on flat ground. Known measured ground impulse is interval input available only after step; not a pure open-loop forecast of future contact, and not proof e is uniquely external force (model error, noise, numerical damping, unmeasured external contact also contribute). Do not call delta-qvel kick physical force in N. Primary H2C sensor proxy: coherent boundary velocity from pelvis local velocimeter+site orientation plus causal backward difference of mass-weighted pelvis-relative COM, including reset derivative zero; refresh kinematics/sensor fields on COPY of boundary state by read-only forward only if verified correct, never regenerate actor observation/RNG, independent deterministic synthetic uniform velocity noise +/-0.1m/s per component keyed by seed and age (same across matched branches, no actor RNG consumption); explicit idealized sim sensor tier, not hardware validated velocity estimator. True centroidal velocity/momentum computed from exact qvel/FK is strictly diagnostic reconciliation upper bound, NEVER detector input. All robot-ground active contact forces summed with verified sign/world frame, no double count, internal self-contact excluded, airborne zero if no external contact; unsupported unknown external contact or invalid fields logged, not silently nominal. No direct injected delta, schedule, matched-OFF state, raw qvel, ground-truth label, or post-future state in detector. At reset no interval exists; report UNINITIALIZED once, score immediately after first completed transition, no startup delay or masking. First push action uses original cached observation and no alarm until measured poststep.

**H2C0 source design pending:** owner EDIT-FIRST opt-in diagnostic H2C in m463_gate3_cp_eval.py, reuse pinned step_fn/action, H2B fork/kick/raw geometry/safety/logging; legacy H2A/B unchanged. Prefer read-only same-action physics replay on copy with exact original applied motor_targets and model, actual prephysics state (including physical kick only for measurement instrument), ten mjx.step substeps, accumulate true ground reaction impulse at correct solver timestamp, verify replay qpos/qvel/time equals original next_state (predeclare tolerance, no second actor/RNG, no branch resampling). Detector receives only contact impulse measurement and coherent boundary proxy, never replay pre-kick velocity/raw state or schedule. Verify contact sign, active efc masks, full robot mass, zero/no support, exact Euler timestep, reset/first step, timestamp coherence, finite values and momentum closure (diagnostic exact tier), all existing source/safety metrics. If force API, alignment, equivalence or closure unsupported, STOP INCONCLUSIVE, not replace with foot force sum or modify pinned source. Source owner local edit/push then SA review before GPU; no new runner/artifact by default. No permission for action/command modification or training.

**Proposed finite H2C1 detector-only protocol (lock exact technical tolerances/seed availability in H2C0 BEFORE execution, separate explicit GO):** one integrated job, fresh candidate development seeds7401/7403 for covariance fit, 7405/7407 for alarm calibration, 7411/7413/7415/7417 for evaluation; 3 profiles stand/forward_fast/turn_left; 12 nominal calibration trajectories total, 12 eval groups each nominal and two double-push left/right forks at299, same .50 step300/.35 step335, max1000. 36 logical eval, max40824 actual environment control transitions incl calibration (12*1000+12*(1000+2*701)); one read-only replay per transition max40824 extra physics-only control intervals, combined max81648 intervals/816480 substeps, refresh forward calls not transitions; no additional probe campaign. Verify no seeds reused in prior evidence before lock; these are development screening, not independent final holdout. All actions original frozen C401M regardless of score. Predictor has no trainable policy. Estimate bias mu and 2x2 covariance S from first six nominal trajectories incl startup, fixed population covariance +1e-6 (m/s)^2 I, score sqrt((e-mu)^T S^-1(e-mu)), threshold tau=max valid score from disjoint six nominal calibration trajectories, alarm strictly score>tau (no tunable entry delay; one rising edge event), freeze artifact+SHA before evaluation. This is empirical nominal calibration not a confidence guarantee or claim of Gaussian residuals. All nominal intervals must be valid and survive, zero evaluation nominal alarms, no score suppression on startup/contact switches. Evaluate detection within first five completed postkick ticks (<=100ms), direction via e-mu dot R_yaw(previous) ^T actual injected XY delta >0 only in offline scoring, match separate rising edge for each event, report signed innovation, latency and exposure; first pushes 24/24 exposed and >=22/24 timely direction-consistent hits; second pushes at least18/24 exposed, >=ceil(.9*exposed) timely direction-consistent hits, nonexposed separately reported (not successes), insufficient second exposure INCONCLUSIVE; events outside windows on pushed trajectories report separately, no assertion all are false alarms. Invalid intervals count against coverage/hits, any nominal invalid or nominal alarm NO-GO/INCONCLUSIVE as appropriate, no threshold adjustment/retry. Paired nominal unchanged and same prefix state/actions, all original-goal tracking/safety diagnostics preserved. No recovery efficacy claim because no control intervention. H2C2 independent holdout only after detector gate PASS and separate GO; H2C3 recovery mapping design only after adequate detector evidence, with separate safety/performance gates. No neural architecture change or PPO. Current decision H2C_R0_DESIGN_COMPLETE / H2C0_DETAILED_SOURCE_DESIGN_PENDING / H2C1_HOLD. H1/H2A/H2B CLOSED, +400M HOLD, Branch B HOLD.

**Offline raw forensic method:** independent NumPy reconstruction of all 180 logical traces from 36 physical groups/117480 finite physical frames, aligned to event steps300/335 and terminal. 36 nominal survivors, 144 pushed traces, 27 failed and117 survived; C/OLD/H3 failures12/6/9. Shared seeds/prefixes/policies make pooled counts descriptive, not independent samples or statistical evidence. Exploratory markers (not acceptance gates, reward proposals or universal detectors): first onset of >=3 consecutive recorded post-last-push ticks height<.60m, no foot-floor contacts, joint/ctrl excess>.001, tilt>20deg; first source terminal contact separately. First-50-tick windows clipped at termination and explicitly account for17/27 shortened failures; no favorable survival selection. All27 failures height<.60 sustained, 0/117 pushed survivors and0/36 nominal; onset median34 ticks after last event (0-based offset), lead to termination median8 ticks/range4..12 (0.08..0.24s). Joint excess26/27 failed vs20/117 survivors (first50:24/27 vs17/117), 26/27 have sustained post-last-push joint marker before height marker, 7 failed traces already had a qualifying joint marker before last push; no assertion joint excursion initiated instability. No-foot-contact18/27 vs1/117; tilt>20deg8/27 vs3/117; ctrl excess22/27 vs116/117 plus36/36 nominal after300, poor standalone discriminator. Ctrl/joint maxima from evaluator actuator_ctrlrange/jnt_range, not specific joint/action saturation causal proof; original per-case safety limits remain. Foot-foot source flag only at final recorded step in all27, negative terminal contact distance (no negative preterminal recorded), precontact pair_present=1 and distance default1.0 not true clearance. Height is root_z not actual CoM. Observed collapse precedes terminal collision on these traces, cannot exclude between-tick contacts/geometry effects, historical H1G includes distinct placement failure without comparable height drop.

**Double-push mechanism:** same-policy single/double share prefix through334; 18/72 doubles fail, 9/18 corresponding singles survive (C4/8,OLD2/4,H3 3/6), other9 singles also fail. No double survives when its corresponding single fails in this matrix. Before push2, 0/72 first-push trajectories achieve 10 consecutive original recovery-band frames by334; only6/72 instantaneously satisfy all bands at334 (all six doubles survive). Median absolute height and tilt deviations vs contemporaneous same-policy nominal at334 in double failures .02165m/6.273deg vs survivors .007663m/1.770deg. Not a universal threshold or causal proof. In pooled standing double cases, same-direction unilateral support at334 yields8 failures/10 versus2/14 BOTH; zero opposite unilateral in this stand subset. C same-side4/4, OLD2/3, H3 2/3 fail, still sparse correlated cases. In same-side stand single-vs-double pairs, observed contralateral first touchdown delayed +5 ticks for C7331/right, C7601/right, OLD7601/right, +6 for H3 7601/right (its single also fails); C7357/left fails with lag0, OLD/H37357/left survive with +1/+2; missing touchdown in some 7613 failures; delayed contact is one possible mechanism, not necessary/sufficient. Turning failures also occur with BOTH and opposite support; no universal stance rule. Across OLD/H3 all48 pushed pairs:39 both survive,6 both fail,3 OLD-only survival,0 H3-only. Median H3-minus-OLD mean XY error in first10 post-last-push frames: both survive -.001720, both fail+.013897, lost3 +.141512m/s; medians common-prefix min-height difference -.001447/-.087555/-.566060m. Cross-policy prepush states not equal; same-policy fork supports incremental effect of second kick, not a causal proof of training mechanism.

**Lost case timing:** H3 seed7601 stand single right push300, prepush height OLD/H3 .76143/.76135 BOTH; sustained ctrl330, joint342, height376, no support379, terminal384; OLD on same prefix ctrl330/joint347 but no height collapse and root .760m at384. H3 seed7357 turn_left double left push335, pre2 height OLD/H3 .71932/.69883 both LEFT support, H3 ctrl338/joint344/height395/no support399/terminal407; OLD retains .755m at407 despite ctrl338/joint351. H3 seed7613 turn_left double left pre2 height .73764/.70291, support OLD LEFT/H3 BOTH, H3 ctrl339/joint344/height392/no support399/terminal404; OLD .754m at404. All 3 H3 terminal root low (.241/.092/.111m); divergence from OLD appears before contact. Observations consistent with insufficiently effective corrective motion, not specific causal action preference or all joint/ctrl excursions causing collapse. No full qpos/qvel/action/actuator vector, actual support loads/CoP/CoM, weighted reward/advantage/gradient in H3 raw report.

**Direct training distribution and collection finding:** both actual +10M reports exact 8192env,32 unroll,batch1024,8 minibatches,5 updates,40 rollouts,6 evals,1 reset/eval,8 rollouts/epoch, C401M fresh Adam, same fingerprint. Pinned Brax training_step scans batch_size*num_minibatches//num_envs=1 unroll/env; 8 training steps per epoch (10485760/(5*262144)), then reset_fn(key_envs) because num_resets_per_eval=1. Instrumenter reset-edge hooks do not remove reset; prior actual OLD/H3 generated-source comparison passed. Hence maximum continuous per-env collection segment256 control ticks=5.12s, even without earlier terminal resets; first/second controlled eval events step300/335 not reachable at those continuous reset ages in this training configuration. Source easy_repeat_support first target age12..24 +<=8 wait, repeat target gap15..30 +<=8 wait, p=.25, same sampled direction, source push off; boundary uses wrapper steps. This establishes a structural state-age distribution mismatch, NOT proof older learned C401M never experienced mature push or all early/late states physically different. Actual event-age histogram not recorded. OLD/H3 46948/46944 events,9267/9299 repeats,1983/2002 repeat gap30..40,214/226 right-only support+gap30..40, only8/8 recorded stand x body-right x right-only support x gap30..40 (0.0863%/0.0860% of repeats), generic 99-post-action milestones31378/31387 not target-conditioned and reset on repeat; motion tag read from post-step command (do not silently equate with pre-event command when refresh occurs). Cannot infer missing left/turn counts from this single measured conjunction. Prior S15 mixed early/late ages200..219 had no repeats, 3.932M unmatched budget, no direct age histogram and no target double success; H/L5/L6 demonstrated nonzero but rare joint exposure and 3/25 same32 repeat tails>=25 vs24/25 across96, no repeat replay needed. This is not a new claim that timing alone works; proposed next strategy differs from S15 by retaining repeat and measured joint exposure, adequate common collection horizon and matched budget.

**Competing hypotheses:** strongest directly supported training deficiency is joint distribution/collection horizon; support recovery and corrective action quality are plausible physical mechanisms. RC1 reward tradeoff not causal; prior H1-M1 height/downward-vz penalty, H1-M2 crossing penalty, H3-M1 stand relaxation all failed equal-budget efficacy/promotion under their protocols; H2A/B blocked before nominal intervention, not evidence external controller ineffectiveness after push. Do not repeat blind penalties. Temporal credit plausible:20/27 failures occur >32 ticks after final push, one >99; original gamma=.98 lambda=.95 pinned defaults and unroll32 (0.64s), truncated GAE uses bootstrapped critic, not proof credit failure; actual TD/advantages unavailable. Actor103 includes local noisy linvel/gyro/gravity/joint angles/vel/last action/phase/command; critic216 adds height/contacts etc; actor lacks explicit contact/root height but inferability/architecture limitation untested. Keep reward/network/observation/GAE/PD/physics unchanged for first hypothesis. No privileged push metadata to actor.

**Selected recommendation, design proposal only:** H4-R0 failure-aware on-policy PPO curriculum (joint exposure, not replay/teacher or H2C), target naturally reached mature gait + short-gap repeated lateral push with standing same-side support as primary vulnerable stratum (both left/right), turning cases monitored; keep broad motion/direction mix, 20% nominal sampler and baseline single/early push coverage. Do not force a support pose, alter physics, sample exact evaluation seeds or discard unfavorable trajectories. One distribution-treatment package, cannot attribute components separately; specify mixture weights, event age/gap, side fallback and achievable target counts before execution, no adaptive threshold tuning. Proposed common collection design for BOTH new control and candidate: same 40-rollout/10485760 budget EACH, C401M fresh Adam, same seed/source/DR/reward/config except common num_evals3 instead of6 (2 epochs x20 unrolls x32=640 continuous ticks, same reset-edge contract/reset1, 40*40=1600 optimizer updates target per arm; fewer checkpoint/eval boundaries and host monitor cadence must be explicitly designed). This is a design alternative, not approved final config, GPU memory/runtime unverified. Example candidate mixture target first age240..300 plus<=8 phase wait, repeat target30..32 plus<=8 side wait (actual30..40); latest age348 gives >=250 theoretical subsequent ticks before 640 cap if survives; source command refresh after step500 means not all five seconds maintain same command, log refresh and same-command tail separately, preserve original command schedule. Existing OLD10M has different reset schedule and cannot be sole causal comparator for common-horizon candidate; new matched original-scheduler control necessary if owner approves this design. C401M/OLD/H3 retained context; no auto-run either arm. Log actual pre-event command/direction/side/age/magnitude/gap, first and repeat counts/attempts/matches/fallback, unique per-event action tail and intervening repeat/termination/reset/command refresh, nominal transition exposure, same-unroll vs continuous tail, finite/GPU. Preserve existing postpush recovery/nominal/safety gates and frozen development protocol as reference; predeclare candidate/control efficacy thresholds before new training (at least prior safety and nominal rigor; ceiling INCONCLUSIVE; later independent holdout separate). Reject if targeted joint exposure not achieved (technical/exposure NO-GO) or controlled recovery fails (efficacy NO-GO). No further probes if current evidence suffices for design. If proposed collection change not feasible, redesign and request approval, never bypass runner budget/reset-edge guards. No larger budget, tuning or automatic H5. H3-R1 offline research complete; H4-R0 detailed training design recommended pending owner approval; no executable changes, no simulation, no PPO. H3-M1 CLOSED, all prior NO-GO intact, H2C/B HOLD.

**Proposed v0 curriculum specification (SA design choices, pending owner review; not approved executable settings):** each natural episode boundary selects one persistent class; 20% nominal disabled (retain original nominal assignment probability), 40% broad (original easy_repeat_support non-disabled scheduler: first age12..24, max wait8, six directions uniform, phase target0/1/2, original .20..60 magnitude range, repeat p=.25, gap15..30 plus max wait8, lateral repeat same-side gate), 16% mature single, 24% mature double. Mature classes first target scheduler zero-based age240..300 inclusive, first original phase target/max wait8, lateral left/right equally sampled for both mature classes, first magnitude uniform .45..55 m/s; mature single stops after first, mature double always schedules same-direction repeat target gap30..32 inclusive plus max wait8 for corresponding actual unilateral support (left=1,right=2); second magnitude uniform .30..40 m/s. Deadline fallback physically injected and logged but NOT support-matched event. All other original reward/PD/termination/action/obs/DR and source command sampling unchanged. This tests a package (timing, class mixture, lateral direction, magnitude, repeat gap/eligibility), not separate causal effects of each component. Broad group still has all directions/motion coverage. Use original control RNG splitting exactly, candidate-only scheduler subkeys from existing scheduler keys without shifting common source RNG progression; no assertion paired random trajectories identical after perturbations. Source command refresh occurs when source info step>500, may change goal before 250-tail; record refresh event even if same sampled value, actual change separately and pre-command vs actor-consumed observation at refresh edge; do NOT extend command period. Source actor action at push transition was sampled from pre-injection obs, count subsequent actions from next transition; termination may consume an action but does not qualify as a fully surviving recovery window.

Nominal assignment not equal nominal transition fraction. Proposed numerical engineering exposure floor for candidate across full run: >=32 actual mature stand same-direction matched repeat events per side with specified age/gap/magnitude bands, >=16 distinct qualifying repeat events per side with >=100 subsequent executed actions and >=8 per side with >=250 subsequent executed actions; terminal cases remain in all denominators, separately report full-window survivors; >=32 mature repeat actual events per turn command x lateral direction (no support-match requirement for this secondary cell), both directions and all 6 broad motion/direction categories nonzero, >=1000 broad push events, nominal transition fraction >=.15 and >=.8 times matched control, original general coverage gates retained. Floors are proposed practical design targets, not statistically derived sample size or evidence of sufficiency; before final execution lock planned allocation, arm-specific early feasibility gate and denominators in H4-M1-0 design review, no post-data relaxation. With 16384 initial reset slots across two epochs, expected planned mature-double stand left/right ~327.68 each before natural terminations/phase fallback (8192*2*.24/6/2); this is assignment arithmetic, NOT guaranteed actual exposure, excludes extra autoresets and assumes uniform commands. 32 per side is roughly one-tenth of planned initial slots, not a causal effect-size estimate. If low exposure, record mechanism (not reached, support fallback, terminal, epoch reset, command refresh), distinguish insufficient design from genuine recovery failure; no automatic extra run.

**Proposed decision gates:** same v1.2.54/55 rigor vs NEW matched control, stand double survival and matched-single5s recovery both >=+2/8, comparator ceiling INCONCLUSIVE absent hard NO_GO; no lost single survival, no lost moving double survival, moving double5s recovery net nonworse each profile, nominal12/12 for both, nominal XY/yaw and standing posture/XY/yaw +5% or .0005 if control<.01, swing +.02, required common standing windows, common prefix joint/ctrl/force excess .001/.001/.1, no new critical terminal flag or nonfinite. ## 5. Fall / Get-up / Resume — tiêu chí bắt buộc mới

**Tuyệt đối không trộn hai bài toán:** `push survival / recovery khi còn đứng` khác `tự đứng dậy từ trạng thái đã ngã`. PPO locomotion hiện tại có `termination` và auto-reset; việc học get-up đòi môi trường cho phép trạng thái sau ngã tiến triển qua nhiều bước, có dữ liệu quan sát và action hợp lệ trong các tư thế ngã.

**Đường triển khai có gate:**

- **F0 — Fall forensics:** kiểm tra điều kiện `done`, root height, body tilt, support/contact, torques, joint limits; phân loại ngã thật vs termination khác. Thu tập fallen states hợp lệ, không lưu/sử dụng reset như recovery. Chỉ đọc/đánh giá, không thay locomotion reward.
- **F1 — Get-up physics feasibility:** trong sandbox thí nghiệm riêng, thử điều khiển deterministic/trajectory hoặc chính sách get-up xuất phát từ nhiều tư thế ngã; kiểm tra quan sát/action, mô-men, contact và tự nâng thân; không nhảy tư thế. Chứng minh 1 ca sấp + 1 ca ngửa có điều khiển trước khi RL quy mô lớn.
- **F2 — Get-up learning:** huấn luyện riêng nếu F1 PASS và VRAM fits, có curriculum từ tư thế ngã ít khó đến khó, đa dạng pose/terrain/contact. Đánh giá xác suất đứng dậy, thời gian get-up, torque, va chạm và stability, không chỉ reward.
- **F3 — Integrated resume:** đẩy → ngã thật → GET_UP → REORIENT → tiếp tục lệnh; **không auto-reset giữa các pha** trong các episode được tính thành công. Giữ command goal qua quá trình đứng dậy, có watchdog/thời gian thất bại được ghi riêng. Mỗi tập phải ghi trạng thái chuyển tiếp và mục đích termination/abort.
- **F4 — Robustness / sim-to-sim:** MJX GPU → MuJoCo CPU, cùng trạng thái đầu/lệnh/kịch bản khi khả thi; kiểm tra sensitivity của ground friction/PD/contact và rõ ràng domain randomization, không suy thành hardware validation.

**Định nghĩa thành công get-up (đề xuất, phải hiệu chỉnh bằng baseline kiểm thử):** từ fallen pose thật, sử dụng torque/action liên tục trong mô phỏng, robot lên upright, đứng ổn định trong một khoảng kiểm tra cố định, sau đó đạt tracking command trong cửa sổ đánh giá riêng. Ba sự kiện `(GET_UP_SUCCESS, STABILIZATION_SUCCESS, RESUME_SUCCESS)` phải được ghi **riêng**; chỉ ghi hoàn thành chu trình khi cả ba đạt và không có teleport/reset.

## 6. Hệ đo và so sánh — bắt buộc cho nghiệm thu

**Training-side:** count push trong PPO transitions, unique episodes có push, category *joint histogram*, thực tế phase target vs phase actual, push mạnh/yếu, event exposure, số bước sau push trước reset/rollout, done theo nguyên nhân, VRAM peak, checkpoint SHA, finite gradients/weights, optimizer state.

**Nominal:** survival horizon, forward/backward/lateral/yaw command tracking RMSE, chiều cao, tilt, joint/foot motion, tốc độ chuyển đổi lệnh, hội tụ khi đứng yên. Không kết luận từ `eval reward` 2 env.

**Push:** ghép **ON/OFF từ cùng full pre-push state trong từng policy**; same evaluation reset seeds + command + push directions/magnitudes/timings giữa policy. Ghi số lần ngã, first-fall time, sống sót 2s/5s/to horizon, matched-OFF recovery 2s/5s, thời gian quay về lệnh, RMSE **cửa sổ bằng nhau**; nếu tử vong trước cửa sổ, ghi censored/fail, không so RMSE 50 bước với 700 bước. Khác policy có thể có full physical state khác dù reset seed cùng.

**Get-up:** tách initial fallen pose distribution và push-induced fall distribution; tỷ lệ tự đứng dậy theo từng pose/dose/command, time-to-upright, stable-time, torque/collision cost và **resume tracking**. Cần đánh giá failure/timeout rõ ràng và không auto-reset; một fall là một thử nghiệm get-up thật, không phải khởi tạo đứng lại.

**Khoa học:** các reset seeds development không độc lập giữa các kịch bản lặp; không dùng 72 lượt từ ba seeds làm 72 mẫu độc lập. Khi tuyên bố cải thiện, cần freeze tập held-out độc lập, đối chứng cùng ngân sách/seed và có nguồn paper rõ. Phân biệt **technical PASS**, **exposure PASS**, **nominal gait PASS**, **robustness PASS** và **get-up/resume PASS**; một loại không suy ra loại khác.

## 7. Quy trình bắt buộc cho MỖI task/thử nghiệm

**Trước task:**

1. Đọc file này và trạng thái cuối `CURRENT STATE`; ghi `task_id`, giả thuyết, năng lực mục tiêu (`PUSH`, `FALL`, `GET_UP`, `RESUME`), input files, branch/commit, checkpoint SHA, source SHA, venv/VRAM, seed và đánh giá **GO/NO-GO**.
2. Tìm/mở các script, evaluator, log/report đã có; **ưu tiên sửa tối thiểu file cũ**; không chạy lại bằng chứng đã đủ. Xác định một biến độc lập cần thay, đối chứng và tiêu chí dừng trước khi xem kết quả.
3. Với mọi thay đổi reward/termination/PD/action/observation/model: **STOP mặc định**. Chỉ thực hiện ở experimental get-up environment khi có biện minh, diff, kế hoạch ablation, test, và không ảnh hưởng baseline/pinned source.

**Trong task:**

4. Theo thứ tự `static inspect → plan → JAX read-only probe trên backend G1 đã xác minh (GPU/CUDA) → MJX full transition → GPU PPO smoke khi được duyệt riêng → evaluation fixed seeds → pilot` và không bỏ qua các gate. **Không tự ý áp dụng CPU probe** trên runtime `g1_m463` chỉ có CUDA; mọi bài kiểm tra nhỏ đều phải phân biệt với PPO training. Chỉ một GPU job nặng cùng lúc, theo dõi VRAM và lỗi/NaN. Bất cứ lỗi import / NVCC / state shape / Orbax nào phải sửa tại đúng tầng, ưu tiên mã cũ, không tạo chuỗi script workaround mới.
5. Không train mới hay nâng difficulty chỉ để lấy reward tốt. Thống kê exposure phải đo trên dữ liệu PPO. Mọi checkpoint/output dùng timestamp và tạo mới, không overwrite artifacts nguyên gốc; log cả stderr/stdout.
6. Nếu xuất hiện NO-GO (OOM, dữ liệu không đủ, SHA mismatch, nonfinite, suy giảm nominal rõ, phase/push không xuất hiện, sai pairing, get-up bằng reset), giữ file lỗi và báo cáo, **không diễn giải thành PASS**.

**Sau task:**

7. Đối chiếu JSON + log + các chỉ số; phân biệt `OBSERVED / INFERRED / UNTESTED`. Viết `result`, `limits`, checkpoint/hash, evidence files, quyết định **GO/NO-GO** và hành động cụ thể tiếp theo. Không khẳng định cải thiện chỉ từ training reward, push count hoặc một seed.
8. Cập nhật `CURRENT STATE`, thêm **1 dòng** vào `TASK LEDGER` và `CHANGELOG` khi có thay đổi rules/mục tiêu, bump version. Commit **chỉ file có ý nghĩa** lên nhánh `experiment/b1-hardware-scaled`, không push secrets, cache, huge checkpoint/video hoặc thay `main`/baseline; lưu SHA commit và đường dẫn báo cáo.
   **Khi task có sửa source code**, áp dụng quy tắc SOURCE-ON-GIT v1.2.6 ở dưới: owner đồng bộ commit lên đúng nhánh và SA review SHA/remote code trước khi dùng cho bước kế tiếp; không push với probe chỉ đọc.
9. Khi trả lời người dùng: ghi rõ đã/chưa chạy, bằng chứng được kiểm tra, còn thiếu gì, lệnh chạy có sẵn, nguồn rủi ro và bước thử **nhỏ nhất đủ trả lời câu hỏi**.

### Quy tắc EDIT-FIRST — sửa file hiện có trước, chỉ tạo file mới khi cần (v1.1.0)

**Mặc định: KHÔNG tạo script, runner, audit, runbook, ZIP, Markdown hoặc artifact mới cho mỗi task.** Ưu tiên đọc lại source + script/evaluator/audit đang sử dụng và **đề xuất cho người dùng tự sửa** số ít dòng nhất tại đúng tầng xảy ra vấn đề. Một task có thể chỉ cần hướng dẫn sửa trực tiếp trong câu trả lời; không cần deliverable mới.

**Trước khi đề xuất thay đổi code, bắt buộc trình bày:** (1) tên file và vị trí/hàm/đoạn có thể tìm bằng `grep`; (2) code đang có và code thay thế hoặc unified diff ngắn; (3) lý do nhân–quả, giả thuyết và bằng chứng nguồn; (4) biến được thay và các biến cố định; (5) tác dụng phụ, độ tương thích source/checkpoint/audit; (6) lệnh syntax/unit/plan/smoke test, tiêu chí PASS/NO-GO và rollback; (7) cần chạy lại training hay chỉ re-audit kết quả cũ. **Không được mặc định sinh toàn bộ file khi một patch vài dòng đã đủ.**

**Chỉ tạo file mới** khi có nhu cầu độc lập thực sự: (a) tách môi trường get-up khỏi source locomotion gốc; (b) yêu cầu artifact có cấu trúc (định dạng báo cáo/manifest/chứng cứ không thể truyền qua chat hoặc log hiện có); (c) quy mô thay đổi lớn, rủi ro mất code, hoặc cần kiểm thử cách ly; (d) người dùng yêu cầu file. Khi tạo, giải thích tại sao sửa file cũ không phù hợp, phạm vi tối thiểu, đặt tên tái sử dụng và tránh tạo nhiều bản `*_v2`, `*_fixed`, `*_hotfix` cho cùng một chức năng. Artifact từ lần chạy do người dùng chủ động sinh (log/report/checkpoint) không bị cấm; nhưng không được phát sinh thêm file hướng dẫn/code lặp lại không cần thiết.

**Không tự ý ghi đè repo trên GitHub** chỉ để sửa source huấn luyện; mặc định gửi hướng dẫn để người dùng tự chỉnh và kiểm thử. Ngoại lệ: người dùng đã yêu cầu **cập nhật rules hiện hành**, hoặc xác nhận muốn assistant sửa repo. Bất kỳ thay đổi nào phải giữ baseline pin và có đường rollback. Nếu phát hiện lỗi giả định/mô tả từ task trước, đính chính công khai trong CURRENT STATE/TASK LEDGER, không tiếp tục dựa trên kết luận cũ.

### Quy tắc SOURCE-ON-GIT — đồng bộ code khi thay đổi trước khi SA review/train (v1.2.6)

**Owner-requested mandatory workflow (2026-11-22):** Sau mỗi **thay đổi source code có ý nghĩa** của dự án G1 (environment/scheduler, Brax instrumentation, PPO runner, evaluator, helper hoặc cấu hình ảnh hưởng hành vi), người thực hiện phải **review diff, commit và push phiên bản source lên** `experiment/b1-hardware-scaled` **trước khi yêu cầu SA kiểm tra lại hoặc dùng source đó cho gate training/evaluation kế tiếp**. SA phải fetch/read đúng GitHub branch/commit và báo SHA, thay vì giả định file local khác phiên bản trên remote. Không yêu cầu push cho các lệnh chỉ đọc, heredoc/probe không ghi file, hoặc artifact thử nghiệm không sửa code. Nếu đang trong một chỉnh sửa dở dang, gom các thay đổi liên quan thành commit có ý nghĩa sau khi kiểm tra, không commit từng thao tác nhỏ; **không thêm task chỉ để push**.

**Owner chạy Git trên máy local; SA không tự ý stage/overwrite/push source.** Trước khi push: `git branch --show-current` phải là `experiment/b1-hardware-scaled`; `git status --short`, `git fetch origin`, kiểm tra có commit mới trên remote. Nếu branch local sạch hoặc có thể fast-forward an toàn, `git pull --ff-only origin experiment/b1-hardware-scaled` **trước khi commit/push**; nếu có sửa Rules/source local, staged work hoặc non-fast-forward conflict, **STOP và đối chiếu diff/log**, không `--force`, không tự reset/rebase/merge/pull gây mất dữ liệu. Rules do SA commit từ GitHub phải được đồng bộ về local một cách an toàn trước khi tạo commit kế tiếp.

**Stage chính xác theo allowlist file source của task** (ví dụ `git add -- m463_coverage_env.py m463_instrument_brax.py m463_push_ppo_train.py`), **không dùng `git add .`, `git add -A` mặc định**. Kiểm tra `git diff --cached --check`, `git diff --cached --stat`, `git diff --cached --name-only`, xem diff của code rồi mới commit. Nếu có file staged ngoài phạm vi, dừng và unstage có chọn lọc; không đưa lên GitHub checkpoint `*.msgpack`, `*.ckpt`, video, output GPU/log, dữ liệu nhạy cảm, `.env`, khóa, token, venv, cache hoặc file lớn; `.gitignore` không thay thế việc review danh sách staged. Giữ nguyên `main`, pinned Playground/Brax, baseline tag và checkpoint; dùng commit message có task ID và lý do kỹ thuật. Push normal `git push -u origin experiment/b1-hardware-scaled` (hoặc `git push` khi đã có upstream), **không force push**.

**Evidence bắt buộc khi bàn giao code:** branch, `git rev-parse HEAD`, danh sách file thay đổi trong commit (`git show --stat --oneline HEAD`), `git status --short`, và `sha256sum` của những file source mục tiêu; xác minh remote HEAD trùng local commit sau push. SA đọc chính commit đó và cập nhật quy chiếu `SOURCE_COMMIT/SHA` trong task. Nếu mã đã sửa đổi sau commit hoặc GitHub chưa nhận push thì **KHÔNG báo source verified** và **KHÔNG chạy PPO trên phiên bản chưa kiểm tra**. Checkpoint/output/training artifact tiếp tục được lưu riêng theo §7, không push mặc định. Khi phiên bản source thay đổi, các SHA pin của thử nghiệm cũ chỉ mô tả **bản cũ**, không tự ý sửa lịch sử hoặc gọi PASS trước đó là PASS cho source mới.

### Mẫu biên bản task (copy vào báo cáo hoặc phần TASK LEDGER rút gọn)

```text
TASK_ID: M4.xx
OBJECTIVE: PUSH | FALL | GET_UP | RESUME
HYPOTHESIS:
BASELINE / CHECKPOINT_SHA:
SOURCE_COMMIT / BRAX_SHA / BRANCH:
CHANGE (independent variable):
CONTROLS (fixed):
SEEDS / COMMANDS / PUSH / FALL_POSES:
BUDGET / GPU_GATE / STOP_CRITERIA:
FILES / LOGS / REPORT_SHA:
RESULT: OBSERVED | INFERRED | UNTESTED
NOMINAL_GAIT / PUSH_EXPOSURE / RECOVERY / GET_UP / RESUME:
DECISION: GO | NO-GO | CONDITIONAL
NEXT_MINIMUM_EXPERIMENT:
RULES_VERSION_USED:
```

### Quy tắc bắt buộc: JAX/CUDA runtime preflight, nguồn local và chặn lặp lỗi (v1.2.1)

1. **Dùng backend G1 đã được kiểm chứng, không tự chọn CPU.** Với `g1_m463` trong venv JAX 0.4.35 / CUDA GPU, máy owner thực tế in `Default backend: gpu`, `Available devices: [CudaDevice(id=0)]`, `Available backends: ['cuda']`. Các lệnh kiểm thử nhỏ cũng chạy trên **GPU** bằng `g1_m463 - <<'PY'` và `jax.jit(fn)`; không thêm `JAX_PLATFORMS=cpu`, `jax.devices('cpu')`, hay ép CPU chỉ vì muốn tiết kiệm tài nguyên. Nếu mục tiêu thực sự đòi CPU, phải có gate xác nhận CPU backend trước, không suy diễn từ môi trường ngoài. JAX JIT biểu thức nhỏ **không phải PPO/MJX training**.
2. **NVCC patch và import order không được thiếu.** Script G1 evaluator đã thành công dùng sibling module `BASE/'G1_M4_0_G1_PPO_ONE_UPDATE_SMOKE_V1'/nvcc_compat.py`. Khi chạy tại repo `G1_PAPER_REPRO`, phải dùng `compat_dir=Path.cwd().parent/'G1_M4_0_G1_PPO_ONE_UPDATE_SMOKE_V1'`, kiểm tra file tồn tại rồi `sys.path.insert(0,str(compat_dir))`; `from nvcc_compat import apply; apply()` **trước bất kỳ** `import jax`, `jax.numpy` hoặc import môi trường gián tiếp dùng JAX. Không mặc định `nvcc_compat` nằm trong repo/root của PYTHONPATH. Nếu file không tồn tại, STOP và đối chiếu bootstrap evaluator đã chạy được; không cài lại JAX/CUDA hoặc tạo module giả.
3. **Đọc traceback theo tầng.** (a) `TypeError` từ `pathlib.Path(cuda_nvcc.__file__)` khi `import jax` nghĩa là bỏ qua compatibility pre-import; (b) `ModuleNotFoundError: nvcc_compat` nghĩa là chưa có sibling path; (c) sau `NVCC_COMPAT metadata_attribute_patched=True`, JAX import và basic JIT thành công nhưng `assert jax.default_backend() == 'cpu'` thất bại thì **chỉ là giả định backend sai**; (d) `Unknown backend cpu. Available backends are ['cuda']` nghĩa là không có CPU backend, **không phải lỗi policy/scheduler**. Không thêm workaround vào PPO source để xử lý các lỗi launch/preflight này.
4. **Không đoán API/JAX decorator.** Với JAX 0.4.35, ưu tiên `jax.jit(fn)`; nếu có gate hợp lệ cần device tường minh dùng `jax.jit(fn,device=device)` hoặc `@functools.partial(jax.jit,device=device)`, không sử dụng `@jax.jit(device=device)` trực tiếp. Kiểm tra `jax.__version__`, `jax.default_backend()`, `jax.devices()` trước khi cung cấp lệnh phụ thuộc thiết bị.
5. **Giữ thứ tự và mức độ bằng chứng.** `py_compile/AST` ⇒ static source expression ⇒ `jax.jit` biểu thức **trên source local chính xác** ⇒ MJX full transition/physical foot contact ⇒ Brax extras reconciliation ⇒ GPU PPO smoke khi được duyệt riêng ⇒ paired nominal/ON/OFF evaluation. Không gọi 12 JAX expression cases là physical MuJoCo PASS hay PPO exposure. Nếu một preflight dừng trước compile/transition, ghi BLOCKED tại đúng tầng, không suy diễn lỗi thuật toán; không chạy PPO chỉ để chứng minh import.
6. **Source và git được bảo vệ.** `git diff` rỗng đối với file *untracked* không chứng minh file chưa đổi. Sử dụng `sha256sum`, kiểm tra byte/diff trực tiếp các bản được upload; không tự ý `git add`, ghi đè local, sửa `main`, thay pinned source/reward/PD/physics. Lỗi M4.70-J: `audit_coverage` được phép cộng **event counts**, nhưng các **tỷ lệ** cần chuẩn hóa theo số rollout đã quan sát; không chỉnh lại lịch sử bất biến hoặc diễn giải tỷ lệ bị cộng dồn như giá trị thật.
7. **Kiểm tra command trước khi giao.** Tái dùng đường dẫn compatibility/launcher từ evaluator thực sự PASS, xác nhận script có đủ import và device, đúng cú pháp Python cho pinned JAX; cung cấp **một lệnh đầy đủ copy-paste**, không bắt user ghép những mảnh sửa chồng chéo từ nhiều tin nhắn. Nếu dừng, cung cấp traceback và status, không đoán kết quả hay đề xuất reinstall.

8. **Lệnh hoàn chỉnh và chốt SHA trước JAX/GPU — phòng tránh lỗi thực thi lặp.** Trước khi gửi bất kỳ heredoc JAX/MJX nào, phải (i) tái sử dụng pinned `g1_m463` đã chạy được; (ii) bảo đảm `cd ~/nguyenbui/humanoid/G1_PAPER_REPRO`; (iii) kiểm tra SHA-256 của **đúng file local** sẽ trích AST/test, không lấy SHA bản upload cũ làm bằng chứng hiện hành; (iv) tìm và `apply()` sibling NVCC patch trước JAX; (v) in và khẳng định backend CUDA/GPU có sẵn, không ép CPU; (vi) cung cấp **một lệnh tự đủ** gồm import, logic, assertions và sentinel PASS; (vii) không gọi kết quả PASS trước khi nhận stdout user. Nếu một bước trước JIT fail, chỉ sửa shell/test harness đúng bước đó, giữ nguyên thuật toán và không khởi chạy PPO để thử môi trường.

9. **M4.70 repeated-evaluator NVCC/JAX process boundary and +400M preparation gate (v1.2.10, 2026-11-22):** `m435_push_ab_stability.run()` executes `nvcc_compat.apply()` before importing JAX. Its compatibility shim intentionally rejects a second `apply()` after JAX has already been imported into the **same Python interpreter**: `RuntimeError('JAX_ALREADY_IMPORTED_COMPAT_MUST_RUN_FIRST')`. **Never put repeated calls to `m435.run()` across seeds in one `g1_m463 - <<'PY'` session** and never call `nvcc_compat.apply()` again in that session after JAX import. Instead launch **one fresh Python interpreter per seed** (e.g., shell `for SEED ...; do g1_m463 - "$SEED" <<'PY' ... PY; done`), keeping the parent/aggregator JAX-free; each child invokes `m435.run()` just once, so it applies NVCC compatibility before its first JAX import. Reuse existing per-seed JSON only after validating checkpoint SHA, seed, case set, ON/OFF completeness, horizon and status; stop on missing/failed cases. This is a **test harness/process-lifetime error**, not evidence that CUDA, PPO or the policy is defective. Do not patch site-packages, weaken `nvcc_compat.py`, or create duplicate evaluator files. **+400M is PREPARATION-ONLY, NOT TRAIN AUTHORIZATION:** current runner `m463_push_ppo_train.py` supports only `--independent-budget {10M,50M,100M}` and caps independent execution at 384 rollouts / 49 evals. `--independent-budget` cannot accompany `--mode plan`; do not work around this with manual `--rollouts/--num-evals` or by launching 4×100M processes and calling it a single +400M run. The prospective 8-rollout/epoch-aligned budget nearest above +400M is **1528 rollouts = 400,556,032 environment steps, 192 eval calls**; this is arithmetic **only**, not implemented or approved. Require separate owner GO, documented minimal EDIT-FIRST runner change and SOURCE-ON-GIT review, resources, checkpoint/restore provenance and evaluation/nominal safety gates before any +400M PPO. Preserve all completed +100M reports, logs, checkpoints and source unchanged.

**Nhật ký lỗi thao tác M4.70-K (tất cả đã biết từ stdout owner, KHÔNG phải lỗi policy):**

| Lỗi đã gặp | Nguyên nhân trực tiếp | Quy tắc tránh tái diễn |
|---|---|---|
| `TypeError ... pathlib.Path(cuda_nvcc.__file__) ... NoneType` ngay khi `import jax` | Thiếu `nvcc_compat.apply()` trước import JAX | Bootstrap NVCC trước mọi import JAX hoặc import gián tiếp |
| `ModuleNotFoundError: nvcc_compat` | Không thêm sibling `G1_M4_0_G1_PPO_ONE_UPDATE_SMOKE_V1` vào `sys.path` | Lấy đường dẫn từ evaluator C/P đã PASS, assert file tồn tại |
| `AssertionError` khi `jax.default_backend() == 'cpu'` | Giả định CPU trong wrapper chỉ có CUDA | In `jax.default_backend()` và `jax.devices()`, dùng GPU thực tế |
| `Unknown backend cpu. Available backends are ['cuda']` | Cố gọi `jax.devices('cpu')` trên backend CUDA-only | Cấm ép CPU không có gate; dùng `jax.jit(fn)` trên GPU |
| `git diff` rỗng cho file untracked | Git không quản lý file đó | Xác minh byte bằng `sha256sum` và so sánh source upload, không kết luận diff=0 |
| `JAX_ALREADY_IMPORTED_COMPAT_MUST_RUN_FIRST` on the second `m435.run()` seed | First seed already imported JAX in same Python interpreter; next seed re-calls `nvcc_compat.apply()` | Run **one child interpreter per seed**, avoid importing JAX in parent, reuse only SHA-validated complete prior JSON; never disable compatibility checks |

**Áp dụng riêng M4.70-K:** dùng `g1_m463 - <<'PY'`, sibling `nvcc_compat.apply()` trước import JAX, **GPU mặc định**; trích AST 10 phép gán và 3 side telemetry expressions trực tiếp từ SHA local `aab0cd38...`, chạy 12 cases bằng `jax.jit`, cần `M470K_JAX_SOURCE_EXPR_PASS`. Source static đã được owner xác nhận; GPU expression test và MJX physical transition vẫn **PENDING**. PPO training NO-GO; B ON HOLD.

**Mẫu bootstrap dùng lại cho mọi heredoc JAX/MJX với `g1_m463` (v1.2.3).** Đặt đầu lệnh trước phần phép thử chuyên biệt; kiểm tra đúng SHA của các file đang dùng *trước* đoạn này. Không cần tạo script mới và tuyệt đối không ép CPU:

### L2 direct-MJX state.info guard (v1.2.5, 2026-11-22)

**Wrapper-vs-direct env rule:** Khi gọi trực tiếp `CoverageJoystick.reset/step` (không có BraxAutoResetWrapper), **KHÔNG** giả định `state.info['steps']` tồn tại. Scheduler `CoverageJoystick.step` đã dùng `info['m463_age']` khi `steps` không có. Trong diagnostic đọc trực tiếp, kiểm tra khóa thật trên state và dùng `int(state.info['m463_age']) > 0` để nhận diện scheduler đã tiến bước; chỉ dùng `info['steps']` sau khi đã chứng minh wrapper thực sự tạo trường đó. Không thêm `steps` giả, không đổi physics/policy/source để sửa lỗi test harness.

**M4.70-L2 owner evidence:** `M470L2_SOURCE_GATE_PASS`, `PAPER_CONFIG_VERIFIED=YES`, sau đó traceback `KeyError: 'steps'` tại guard do assistant viết `int(state.info['steps']) > 0`; `M470L2_REAL_SUPPORT_FOUND` và matched-side chưa được báo, do vậy **L2 BLOCKED AT HARNESS**, không phải physical-contact FAIL. JAX warnings `overflow encountered in cast` xuất hiện nhưng không xác định là nguyên nhân traceback; giữ log. **EDIT-FIRST fix đúng một expression** trong heredoc L2: thay `int(state.info['steps'])` bằng `int(state.info['m463_age'])`; giữ nguyên rest of probe, seed7601, C401M, command0.2m/s, <=150 steps, duration <=3s, SHA checks, GPU backend, no push scan/control conditions. Không cần thêm file, sửa source hoặc chạy PPO. Sau khi rerun mới ghi nhận left/right matched physical contact, rồi Brax extras gate. B ON HOLD, H unpromoted.

### Lệnh hợp nhất M4.70-K source-expression GPU — bản chuẩn (v1.2.4)

**Phạm vi:** read-only local file inspection + `jax.jit` trên GPU mặc định, **không** MJX physical transition và **không** PPO training. Một lệnh đầy đủ copy/paste thay toàn bộ chuỗi vá lệnh rời rạc trước đây. Mẫu này **chỉ áp dụng cho ba SHA post-edit M4.70-K nêu trong script**, không tái dùng SHA cũ cho task mới; giữ nguyên `g1_m463` và đường dẫn sibling NVCC đã chứng minh. Nếu SHA mismatch thì **STOP** và yêu cầu source local, không tự cập nhật kỳ vọng. Nếu JAX compile hay assertions sai, **STOP** ở source-expression gate; không suy diễn lỗi policy; không tự đổi runtime/CPU. Mỗi case `PASS` là predicate/telemetry *biểu thức* được JIT, chưa xác thực contact MJX hoặc Brax PPO extras.

```bash

g1_m463 - <<'PY'
import ast

from types import SimpleNamespace

root = Path.cwd()

expected = {
    'm463_coverage_env.py': 'aab0cd38ddbd4f9a4ee54f319429065dcf3924b81108f3a3417748f2182e23b1',
    'm463_instrument_brax.py': 'e47c4318c9369f98bf9336bf2320e98d5d40da8f1515aa5839bb9cf2ff442866',
    'm463_push_ppo_train.py': '3f569f3e149987defbe1d10f9a39c40f78b792501c1b21e82db44c4f6c4e006d',
}
for name, sha in expected.items():
    path = root / name
    assert path.is_file(), f'M470K_MISSING_SOURCE: {name}'
    observed = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert observed == sha, f'M470K_SOURCE_SHA_MISMATCH: {name} {observed}'
    ast.parse(path.read_text())
print('M470K_SOURCE_SHA_GATE_PASS')

# NVCC compatibility MUST be applied before ANY JAX import.
compat = root.parent / 'G1_M4_0_G1_PPO_ONE_UPDATE_SMOKE_V1'
assert (compat / 'nvcc_compat.py').is_file(), f'M470K_NO_NVCC_MODULE: {compat}'
sys.path.insert(0, str(compat))
from nvcc_compat import apply
apply()
import jax
import jax.numpy as jnp
assert jax.__version__ == '0.4.35', jax.__version__
assert jax.default_backend() == 'gpu', jax.default_backend()
assert jax.devices(), 'M470K_NO_GPU_DEVICE'
print('M470K_CUDA_BOOTSTRAP_PASS', jax.devices())

source = ast.parse((root / 'm463_coverage_env.py').read_text())
cls = next(n for n in source.body
           if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef) and n.name == 'CoverageJoystick')
step = next(n for n in cls.body
            if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == 'step')
order = ('matched','side_target','side_gate','side_matched',
         'effective_match','deadline','ready','event','repeat','side_event')
assigned = {}
for node in step.body:
    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
        for lhs in node.targets:
            if isinstance(lhs, ast.Name) and lhs.id in order:
                assert lhs.id not in assigned, f'M470K_DUPLICATE_{lhs.id}'
                assigned[lhs.id] = node
assert set(assigned) == set(order), ('M470K_MISSING', set(order)-set(assigned))

updates = [n for n in ast.walk(step)
           if isinstance(n, ast.Call) and isinstance(n.func, ast.Attribute)
           and n.func.attr == 'update'
           and isinstance(n.func.value, ast.Name) and n.func.value.id == 'out']
assert len(updates) == 1, ('M470K_OUT_UPDATE_COUNT', len(updates))
vals = {kw.arg:kw.value for kw in updates[0].keywords}
keys = ('m463_repeat_side_target','m463_repeat_side_matched',
        'm463_repeat_side_fallback')
assert all(k in vals for k in keys), ('M470K_TELEMETRY_MISSING', set(keys)-set(vals))
body = '\n'.join('    '+ast.unparse(assigned[k]).replace('\n','\n    ')
                 for k in order)
code = ('def f(direction,count,phase_target,phase_actual,support_side,'
        'age,next_step,disabled,max_count):\n'+body+
        '\n    return (ready,event,repeat,side_event,side_target,'+
        ','.join(ast.unparse(vals[k]) for k in keys)+')\n')

def make(stage):
    ns = {'jp':jnp,'self':SimpleNamespace(_m463_stage=stage)}
    exec(code, ns)
    return jax.jit(ns['f'])  # pinned GPU default; NO CPU override

cases = [
    ('first_original_phase','easy_repeat_support',3,0,1,1,1,18,17,0,True,0,0,0),
    ('first_original_wait','easy_repeat_support',3,0,1,2,3,18,17,0,False,0,0,0),
    ('right_target_hit','easy_repeat_support',3,1,1,1,2,18,17,0,True,2,1,0),
    ('right_wait_wrong','easy_repeat_support',3,1,1,1,1,18,17,0,False,0,0,0),
    ('right_timeout','easy_repeat_support',3,1,1,1,1,25,17,0,True,2,0,1),
    ('left_target_hit','easy_repeat_support',2,1,1,1,1,18,17,0,True,1,1,0),
    ('left_wait_wrong','easy_repeat_support',2,1,1,1,2,18,17,0,False,0,0,0),
    ('nonlateral_original','easy_repeat_support',0,1,1,1,2,18,17,0,True,0,0,0),
    ('old_stage_unchanged','easy_repeat',3,1,1,1,1,18,17,0,True,0,0,0),
    ('no_push_unchanged','easy_repeat_support',3,1,1,1,2,18,17,1,False,0,0,0),
    ('not_yet_eligible','easy_repeat_support',3,1,1,1,2,16,17,0,False,0,0,0),
    ('third_push_disallowed','easy_repeat_support',3,2,1,1,2,18,17,0,False,0,0,0),
]
cached = {}
for name,stage,d,c,pt,pa,side,age,nxt,disabled,ev,target,match,fb in cases:
    if stage not in cached:
        cached[stage] = make(stage)
    args = (d,c,pt,pa,side,age,nxt,disabled,2)
    result = cached[stage](*[jnp.asarray(v,dtype=jnp.int32) for v in args])
    ready,event,repeat,side_event,side_target,t,m,f = result
    got = (bool(event),int(t),int(m),int(f))
    expected_case = (ev,target,match,fb)
    assert got == expected_case, (name,got,expected_case)
    assert int(m)+int(f) == int(t>0), (name,'bad side count')
    assert int(t) == 0 or bool(repeat), (name,'target without repeat')
    print('PASS',name)
print('M470K_JAX_SOURCE_EXPR_PASS')
PY
```

Không thay đổi mô hình, reward, physics, PPO, baseline, North Star hoặc ưu tiên nhánh A/B.


- **v1.2.1 — 2026-11-22:** Bổ sung quy tắc chặn tái diễn lỗi command/JAX từ M4.70-K: pinned CUDA GPU-only, không ép CPU khi `g1_m463` không hỗ trợ; dùng đúng sibling `nvcc_compat` và chạy `apply()` trước mọi import JAX; kiểm tra backend/JAX decorator; phân tầng nguồn lỗi import vs algorithm vs MJX vs PPO; phát lại **một lệnh đầy đủ** có kiểm tra SHA và điều kiện dừng, không dùng `git diff` rỗng trên untracked như chứng cứ; giữ phân biệt additive counts và rates M4.70-J. Không đổi North Star, Branch A/B, baseline hay nguồn paper.


- **v1.2.0 — 2026-11-21:** Theo yêu cầu người dùng, khóa workstream hiện tại thành **A ONLY** (locomotion + push robustness/recovery). **B/F0–F4 ON HOLD** cho tới chỉ thị chuyển nhánh rõ ràng; vẫn giữ North Star get-up lâu dài. Đánh giá thử nghiệm theo information gain, tách bounded exploratory GO khỏi scientific PASS; không bắt lặp các phép thử tái lập chi phí cao như gate tuyệt đối nếu không quyết định được bước A. Giữ EDIT-FIRST và baseline pin.
- **v1.1.0 — 2026-11-21:** Thêm quy tắc bắt buộc EDIT-FIRST: ưu tiên chỉ rõ file/hàm/diff/lý do/test/rollback để người dùng tự sửa; chỉ tạo file mới khi có nhu cầu độc lập thực sự. Sửa trạng thái bằng chứng M4.65-T1: Brax host reset sau mỗi epoch khi `num_resets_per_eval=1`; 5 rollout cập nhật PPO nhưng không liên tục trạng thái vật lý. Đề xuất thay đúng 2 file runner/audit hiện có; chưa chạy lại GPU.
- **v1.0.0 — 2026-11-21:** Tạo single source-of-truth cho nghiên cứu G1. Mục tiêu mới bắt buộc: push exposure và recovery **cùng** tự đứng dậy sau ngã rồi tiếp tục command. Khóa cấu hình/baseline, phân biệt termination với fall, bổ sung Gate F0–F4, workflow từng task, và tích hợp Gate3b dose0.50 thực tế.

---

**Nguyên tắc bảo trì:** File này được phép tiến hóa có kiểm soát. Khi thông số, mục tiêu hay quy tắc thay đổi: **bump semantic version, ghi lý do + bằng chứng + ngày vào CHANGELOG, cập nhật CURRENT STATE, và commit vào đúng nhánh**. Các task phải khai báo version đã đọc. Nếu kết quả mới chỉ thay đổi số liệu, cập nhật CURRENT STATE/TASK LEDGER, không cần đổi khái niệm hoặc mục tiêu. Nếu thiếu file hoặc SHA, dừng kết luận và yêu cầu đúng bằng chứng thiếu.
